im building a deck for bio-techne clevels regarding high roi ai (/ml) levers for them. should be hgihly rlevant, pnl driven and focus on tpo tier use cases for them that are no brainers and have masive roi and actual improtant relvant impact and value. i want to build 4 cases: 1.1 Precision commercial engine for Protein Sciences Primary segment: Protein Sciences (circa 75 percent of revenue, high margin workhorse) Saxo+1 Primary P\&L impact: revenue, gross margin Dynamic pricing and discount optimization for reagents and instruments AI models optimise list prices and discount corridors by segment, elasticity, competitor intensity and win probability. Next best action for KAMs and inside sales Propensity-to-buy models surface which accounts to call, what to push (kit, reagent, platform) and when, based on order history, pipeline, trial use, web behaviour. Intelligent promotion design and ROI tracking Uplift models test promotions and bundles, focusing spend on offers that actually move incremental volume rather than subsidise base demand. Digital storefront personalisation On bio-techne.com, recommendations and search ranking are tailored to lab type, past orders and protocols, lifting basket size and conversion. \--- 1.3 Account intelligence for strategic and pharma customers Primary P\&L impact: revenue, salesforce productivity 360-degree view and share-of-wallet estimation Models estimate total lab spend by category and brand across big pharma, biotechs and top academic centers, highlighting white space where Bio-Techne is under-penetrated. Deal and contract analytics AI benchmarks global framework agreements and pricing, flags underpriced contracts and renewal risk, and recommends repricing or cross-sell plays. \--- 2.2 Smart network, capacity and SKU management Primary P\&L impact: COGS, working capital SKU complexity and portfolio rationalisation Algorithms cluster SKUs by margin, demand volatility and substitutability to target long tail products for rationalisation or made-to-order strategies. Demand forecasting and inventory positioning Forecasts by SKU, region and channel inform where to hold stock vs build to order, reducing back orders and excess inventory. Production planning for long lead time inputs For critical raw materials and components, AI anticipates demand and supply risk and proposes optimal order timing and safety stock levels. \--- 4.1 Finance, planning and pricing co-pilot Primary P\&L impact: SG and A, gross margin Rolling forecast and scenario engine Models reconcile sales pipeline, orders, macro and historical patterns to automate P and L and cash forecasts, giving CFO and segment leaders earlier line of sight on miss or beat. Price realisation and margin leakage analytics Finance and commercial teams get automated insight into where discounts, freight, rebates or free goods are eroding margins vs policy. \--- based on Quantifica potencial incremental por marca, país e HCP, combinando prescrição, acesso, concorrência e maturidade omnichannel.

2\. Segmentação de Valor de HCP

Segmenta HCPs com base em comportamento real, resposta omnichannel e abertura digital, distinguindo foco intensivo de cobertura eficiente.

3\. ROI e Alocação por Canal

Mede impacto marginal por canal e recomenda alocação ótima de budget entre field, digital e medical."

print out 4 snippets of code that each correspond to each fase which contains 3 each so i can have the 4 slides done:

"\#\# Fase 1 – Priorização Omnichannel

\#\#\# 1\. Potencial de Mercado e Marca

Quantifica o potencial incremental por marca, indicação, país e segmento de HCP, integrando dados de prescrição, acesso, concorrência e maturidade omnichannel para orientar decisões de investimento futuro.

\#\#\# 2\. Segmentação de Valor de HCP

Segmenta HCPs de forma dinâmica com base em comportamento real de prescrição, resposta a interações omnichannel e abertura a canais digitais, distinguindo esforço intensivo de cobertura eficiente.

\#\#\# 3\. ROI e Alocação por Canal

Mede a contribuição marginal de cada canal e recomenda a alocação ótima de budget e esforço entre field, digital e medical.

\---

\#\# Fase 2 – Orquestração de Jornadas

\#\#\# 4\. Desenho de Jornadas Omnichannel

Define jornadas-alvo por segmento de HCP e ciclo de vida da marca, com sequência clara de canais e objetivos clínicos e comerciais.

\#\#\# 5\. Next-Best-Action

Determina, para cada HCP e momento, a próxima ação com maior probabilidade de impacto, incluindo canal, mensagem, intensidade e timing.

\#\#\# 6\. Orquestração Cross-Channel

Assegura coerência e sincronização entre interações humanas e digitais, evitando redundância e mensagens contraditórias.

\---

\#\# Fase 3 – Execução Personalizada

\#\#\# 7\. Conteúdos Modulares

Estrutura conteúdos clínicos e comerciais em módulos reutilizáveis e combináveis, permitindo personalização por HCP, canal e país sem fricção regulatória.

\#\#\# 8\. Copilot de Rep e MSL

Apoia reps e MSLs com insights contextuais antes, durante e após cada interação omnichannel.

\#\#\# 9\. Execução Field e Digital

Otimiza cobertura, frequência e papel do field force em articulação com canais digitais para maximizar impacto incremental.

\---

\#\# Fase 4 – Performance e Expansão de Valor

\#\#\# 10\. Steering Preditivo

Monitoriza indicadores preditivos ligados a outcomes futuros, permitindo correções rápidas e decisões informadas.

\#\#\# 11\. Expansão de Valor por HCP

Identifica oportunidades de crescimento por HCP, incluindo novas indicações, maior profundidade de uso e cross-portfolio.

\#\#\# 12\. Previsão de Churn

Deteta sinais precoces de perda de engagement ou prescrição e ativa ações de retenção antes do impacto em vendas.

\---"

Inventory \&amp; Content Propagation\</p\>" "\<p\>Manual updates, booking errors.\</p\>" "\<p\>Push validated changes across channels to cut errors, accelerate updates, and improve conversion.\</p\>"\] \["\<p\>Reservations \&amp; Front Office\</p\>" "\<p\>RF1. Reservation Changes End-to-End\</p\>" "\<p\>High handling time, inconsistent decisions, rework loops.\</p\>" "\<p\>Execute policy-based changes faster to cut handling time, reduce rework, and improve guest experience.\</p\>"\] \["" "\<p\>RF2. Targeted Pre-Arrival Upsell\</p\>" "\<p\>Under monetized demand, low attach.\</p\>" "\<p\>Orchestrate targeted upsell pre-arrival to increase attach rate, lift margin, and improve experience.\</p\>"\] \["\<p\>In-Stay Guest Experience\</p\>" "\<p\>GX1. Proactive Issue Detection \&amp; Recovery\</p\>" "\<p\>Late issue detection, reactive fixes.\</p\>" "\<meta charset='utf-8'\>Trigger proactive interventions to lift ratings, reduce compensation cost, and raise repeat intent.\<\!-- notionvc: 7997f5e5-f3e5-4cc6-9b08-8515caf5dd8d \--\>"\] \["" "\<p\>GX2. Room Turn \&amp; Housekeeping Dispatch\</p\>" "\<p\>Manual prioritization, readiness delays, QA gaps.\</p\>" "\<p\>Optimize dispatch and exceptions to improve labor productivity and shorten room turn-time.\</p\>"\] \["" "\<p\>OM2. Work Order Triage \&amp; Vendor Coordination\</p\>" "\<p\>Slow triage, repeat incidents.\</p\>" "\<p\>Route tickets to action plans faster to reduce downtime, lower maintenance cost, and improve closure.\</p\>"\] \["\<p\>Finance \&amp; Workforce\</p\>" "\<p\>FW1. and fill it w the following content (adapt the content so it fits the style of the table, i.e. make the text fit the original numer of caracters):

"Finance Control DomainHighest-ROI WorkflowsConcrete Pain Points (Airline-Specific)Agentic Solution & PnL ImpactPlanning & ForecastingPF1. Integrated Rolling Forecast (Ops-to-Finance)Forecasts distorted by disruptions, crew changes, and ad-hoc cost eventsManual overrides without traceabilityWeak confidence in mid-cycle outlook updatesBuild a rolling forecast that ingests disruption events, crew schedules, fleet utilization, and cost drivers to continuously update outlooks, reduce forecast error, and improve credibility of financial guidance.PF2. Driver-Level Forecast ExplainabilityFP\&A teams struggle to explain why forecasts moveDisconnect between operational events and financial impactAutomatically attribute forecast changes to specific drivers (disruptions, crew, fuel, accommodation, inventory movements) to improve transparency and decision confidence.Capital Allocation & Strategic PlanningCA1. Initiative-Level ROI TrackingStrategic initiatives approved on static business casesNo systematic tracking of realized vs. expected returnsTrack initiative performance against original assumptions and reallocate capital away from underperforming programs to protect ROIC.CA2. Fleet & Network Scenario Stress-TestingLong-term decisions exposed to demand and cost volatilityLimited downside scenario analysisSimulate fleet and network economics under demand, fuel, and disruption scenarios to improve resilience and long-term margin outcomes.Revenue & Margin EconomicsRM1. Route Profitability with Disruption Cost AttributionRoute margins distorted by unallocated disruption and accommodation costsDelayed visibility into true route economicsAttribute disruption-driven costs to routes and events to reveal true profitability and improve network and fleet decisions.RM2. Margin Bridge: Plan vs. ActualMargin erosion identified late and at aggregate levelLimited driver-level diagnosticsDecompose margin movements into revenue, cost, and disruption drivers to enable earlier corrective actions.Cost Execution & Financial OperationsCE1. Crew Accommodation Cost Reconciliation (explicitly mentioned)Manual matching of hotel invoices vs. Excel room-night filesValidation of check-in/out dates, contracted rates, taxis, buses, amendmentsHigh risk of leakage and finance effortAutomate reconciliation across aggregator data, hotel contracts, room-night Excel files, and invoices to flag rate, date, and volume mismatches, reduce leakage, and stabilize disruption-related costs.CE2. Direct Operating Cost OCR & Posting (explicitly mentioned)OCR-heavy workflows with manual correctionsDelayed and error-prone posting into SAP or Sky MetricsDeploy airline-specific document intelligence to extract, validate, classify, and post DOC invoices directly into SAP / Sky Metrics, accelerating close and improving cost accuracy.Working Capital & Asset ControlWC1. Headphones Inventory Flow Forecasting (explicitly mentioned)No reliable view of inventory by locationUnclear ownership of outbound flows from central warehouse to outstationsReactive replenishmentForecast consumption and movements by location, track ownership of outbound assets, and give finance real-time visibility to reduce losses, emergency shipments, and excess stock.WC2. Asset Accountability & Replenishment LogicExcess safety stock due to uncertaintyWorking capital tied up unnecessarilyDefine demand-driven replenishment rules to optimize service levels while freeing working capital.Performance Management & GovernancePG1. Cost & Forecast Variance ControlVariances explained manually and lateWeak accountability for recurring deviationsAutomate variance attribution and flag recurring drivers to improve cost discipline and forecast governance.PG2. Contract & Policy Compliance MonitoringInconsistent application of contracts and policiesLimited visibility into exceptionsContinuously monitor compliance with hotel, transport, and supplier contracts to reduce leakage and enforce controls.Finance Decision SupportDS1. FP\&A and Finance Business Partner CopilotFinance time spent reconciling data instead of advisingSlow turnaround on what-if questionsProvide finance teams with copilots that answer concrete questions (cost impact, scenario deltas, sensitivity) to increase decision velocity and finance leverage.DS2. Executive Steering InsightsExecutive reviews dominated by lagging indicatorsLimited early-warning signalsSurface forward-looking risk and opportunity signals to enable proactive steering at ExCo level.Finance Data ArchitectureDA1. SAP-Centric Finance Data Layer (explicitly mentioned)Fragmented operational and finance systemsHigh effort to connect SAP to upstream dataBuild a finance-owned data layer connecting SAP with operational systems to create a single source of truth for forecasting, costing, and reporting.DA2. Reusable Finance Data ProductsEach analytics use case built from scratchNo compounding effectStandardize finance data products (cost drivers, disruptions, inventory flows) to compound value across all AI use cases."

fill this in w the following solution (and adapt content accordingly):

PF1. Integrated Rolling Forecast with Disruption & Crew Signals

A continuous ops-to-finance loop that stabilizes outlooks and restores forecast credibility.

Challenges

Forecasts distorted by disruptions, crew changes, and ad-hoc cost events

Heavy manual overrides with limited traceability

Low confidence in mid-cycle outlook updates and guidance

Solution

Ingest disruption events, crew schedules, fleet utilization, and accommodation drivers into rolling forecasts

Driver-based models that update outlooks continuously, not monthly

Scenario overlays to quantify downside and recovery paths

Full explainability of forecast movements by driver

Impact

Material reduction in forecast error and volatility

Higher confidence in outlooks and guidance discussions

Earlier corrective actions on cost and margin deviations

"RM1. Route Profitability with Disruption Cost Attribution

Reveals true route economics by assigning disruption costs where they belong.

Challenges

Route margins distorted by unallocated disruption and accommodation costs

Delayed visibility into true profitability by route

Strategic decisions made on incomplete economics

Solution

Attribute disruption-driven costs to routes, events, and time windows

Combine revenue, direct operating costs, and disruption costs in a single route view

Surface structural margin issues masked by aggregation

Impact

Clear visibility into true route profitability

Better-informed network and fleet decisions

Reduction of structural losses at route level"

"RM2. Margin Bridge – Plan vs. Actual

Explains why margin moved versus plan and which drivers are responsible.

CHALLENGES

Margin erosion identified late and only at aggregate level

Disruption and crew-related costs blended into broad variances

FP\&A effort spent manually rebuilding explanations for reviews

SOLUTION

Decompose margin vs. plan into revenue, cost, and disruption drivers

Attribute deviations to specific events, periods, and operational causes

Standardize margin bridge logic across forecasts, MBRs, and ExCo materials

IMPACT

Faster, clearer explanation of margin movements

Earlier identification of structural vs. temporary deviations

Stronger"

same, but for this solution now:

"CE1. Crew Accommodation Cost Reconciliation

Automates reconciliation of disruption-driven accommodation spend to eliminate leakage.

CHALLENGES

Manual matching of hotel invoices vs. Excel room-night files

Validation of check-in and check-out dates, contracted rates, taxis, buses, and amendments

High finance effort with persistent overbilling risk

SOLUTION

Reconcile aggregator data, hotel contracts, room-night files, and invoices

Automatically flag rate, date, and volume mismatches

Track accommodation costs by disruption event and period

IMPACT

Direct reduction in accommodation cost leakage

Significant reduction in manual finance workload

Improved predictability of disruption-related spend"
